  const modelContext = navigator.modelContext;
  if (!modelContext) {
    return;
  }

  const toolDefinitions = [{"kind":"docs-search","name":"render.docs.search","description":"Search Render documentation by keyword.","inputSchema":{"type":"object","properties":{"query":{"type":"string","description":"Keywords to search for in the Render docs."}},"required":["query"],"additionalProperties":false},"annotations":{"readOnlyHint":true}},{"kind":"docs-markdown","name":"render.docs.get-markdown","description":"Fetch a Render docs page as markdown by slug.","inputSchema":{"type":"object","properties":{"slug":{"type":"string","description":"Docs page slug without a leading slash. Nested slugs are allowed."}},"required":["slug"],"additionalProperties":false},"annotations":{"readOnlyHint":true}},{"kind":"llms-index","name":"render.llms.get-index","description":"Fetch the Render llms.txt index as markdown.","inputSchema":{"type":"object","properties":{},"additionalProperties":false},"annotations":{"readOnlyHint":true}},{"kind":"blog-index","name":"render.blog.get-index","description":"Fetch the latest Render blog index as markdown (most recent 20 posts).","inputSchema":{"type":"object","properties":{},"additionalProperties":false},"annotations":{"readOnlyHint":true}},{"kind":"articles-index","name":"render.articles.get-index","description":"Fetch the latest Render articles index as markdown (most recent 50 articles).","inputSchema":{"type":"object","properties":{},"additionalProperties":false},"annotations":{"readOnlyHint":true}}];

  const buildStructuredResult = (value) => ({
    content: [
      {
        type: 'text',
        text: typeof value === 'string' ? value : JSON.stringify(value, null, 2),
      },
    ],
    structuredContent: typeof value === 'string' ? { text: value } : value,
  });

  const normalizeSlug = (value) =>
    String(value ?? '')
      .trim()
      .replace(/^\/+/, '')
      .replace(/\.md$/i, '');

  const encodeSlug = (slug) =>
    slug
      .split('/')
      .filter(Boolean)
      .map((segment) => encodeURIComponent(segment))
      .join('/');

  const fetchJson = async (path) => {
    const response = await fetch(path, {
      headers: {
        Accept: 'application/json',
      },
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Request failed with status ${response.status} for ${path}`);
    }

    return response.json();
  };

  const fetchText = async (path) => {
    const response = await fetch(path, {
      headers: {
        Accept: 'text/markdown, text/plain;q=0.9',
      },
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Request failed with status ${response.status} for ${path}`);
    }

    return response.text();
  };

  const tools = toolDefinitions.map((tool) => ({
    name: tool.name,
    description: tool.description,
    inputSchema: tool.inputSchema,
    annotations: tool.annotations,
    execute: async (input) => {
      switch (tool.kind) {
        case 'docs-search': {
          const query = String(input?.query ?? '').trim();
          if (!query) {
            throw new Error('The "query" field is required.');
          }

          const result = await fetchJson('/api/agent/docs-search?query=' + encodeURIComponent(query));
          return buildStructuredResult(result);
        }
        case 'docs-markdown': {
          const slug = normalizeSlug(input?.slug);
          if (!slug) {
            throw new Error('The "slug" field is required.');
          }

          const markdown = await fetchText('/docs/' + encodeSlug(slug) + '.md');
          return buildStructuredResult({
            slug,
            markdown,
          });
        }
        case 'llms-index': {
          const markdown = await fetchText('/llms.txt');
          return buildStructuredResult({
            path: '/llms.txt',
            markdown,
          });
        }
        case 'blog-index': {
          const markdown = await fetchText('/blog.md');
          return buildStructuredResult({
            path: '/blog.md',
            markdown,
          });
        }
        case 'articles-index': {
          const markdown = await fetchText('/articles.md');
          return buildStructuredResult({
            path: '/articles.md',
            markdown,
          });
        }
        default: {
          throw new Error('Unsupported WebMCP tool.');
        }
      }
    },
  }));

  if (typeof modelContext.registerTool === 'function') {
    for (const tool of tools) {
      try {
        modelContext.registerTool(tool);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Failed to register WebMCP tool', tool.name, error);
      }
    }
    return;
  }

  if (typeof modelContext.provideContext === 'function') {
    modelContext.provideContext({ tools });
  }

class="col-span-3"></div><div class="col-span-13 border-l-1 pb-50 lg:pb-[120px]"><div class="w-full p-20 pt-100 lg:border-b-1 lg:pt-[200px]"><h2 class="typography text-heading-lg-alternate font-roobert max-w-[300px] lg:max-w-[unset]">Frequently Asked Questions</h2></div><div class="w-full px-15 lg:grid lg:grid-cols-13 lg:p-0 lg:pt-[70px]"><div class="col-span-3 hidden lg:block"></div><div class="flex flex-col gap-x-50 gap-y-60 lg:col-span-10 lg:grid lg:grid-cols-2 lg:gap-y-[120px] lg:pr-[5vw]"><div class="flex flex-col gap-20"><h3 class="typography text-body-xl font-montreal">How does billing work?</h3><div class="text-[16px]"><p>Render charges for three things: your workspace plan (flat subscription), metered features like bandwidth (usage-based), and <a class="underline" href="https://render.com/pricing#compute">compute</a> for your applications (usage-based). </p></div></div><div class="flex flex-col gap-20"><h3 class="typography text-body-xl font-montreal">How am I billed for compute?</h3><div class="text-[16px]"><p><a class="underline" href="https://render.com/pricing#compute">Compute plans</a> are set at a per-service basis, and billing is prorated to the second. If you create a web service on a paid compute plan and turn it off 24 hours later, you are only billed for 24 hours of compute. This also applies to service types that spin up and shut down automatically, like Cron Jobs and Workflows. You are only billed for compute when these services are actively running your workload.</p></div></div><div class="flex flex-col gap-20"><h3 class="typography text-body-xl font-montreal">My Pro or Scale workspace did not have any activity or services this month. Will I still be charged for the workspace subscription? </h3><div class="text-[16px]"><p>If a workspace has no services (live or suspended) and no activity during a month, the workspace subscription fee for that month will be waived.</p></div></div><div class="flex flex-col gap-20"><h3 class="typography text-body-xl font-montreal">I run a large agency with developers on multiple teams. What does this mean for us?</h3><div class="text-[16px]"><p>Scale and Enterprise plans let you create multiple workspaces within the same organization to better manage multiple teams. Our team is happy to help you determine the best way to organize your teams on Render. <a class="underline" href="https://render.com/contact">Contact us</a> here.</p></div></div><div class="flex flex-col gap-20"><h3 class="typography text-body-xl font-montreal">What are build pipeline minutes, and how can I track my usage?</h3><div class="text-[16px]"><p>Your workspace&#x27;s builds and other pre-deploy tasks use Render compute resources. <a class="underline" href="https://docs.render.com/build-pipeline#pipeline-minutes">Pipeline minutes</a> track the duration of these tasks. You can view your pipeline usage from your dashboard&#x27;s Billing page.</p><p>With a Professional plan or higher, you can enable the <a class="underline" href="https://docs.render.com/build-pipeline#pipeline-tiers">Performance pipeline</a> to run builds on larger compute instances, with an additional corresponding charge for pipeline minutes.</p></div></div><div class="flex flex-col gap-20"><h3 class="typography text-body-xl font-montreal">How do free compute plans work?</h3><div class="text-[16px]"><p>With Render’s <a class="underline" href="https://render.com/docs/free">Free compute plans</a>, you can spin up <a class="underline" href="https://docs.render.com/free#free-web-services">web services</a>, <a class="underline" href="https://docs.render.com/free#free-key-value">Render Key Value instances</a>, and <a class="underline" href="https://render.com/docs/free#free-postgres">Render Postgres databases</a> at no charge. Free compute plans types have usage limits and are designed to help you to explore new tech, build personal projects, and preview Render&#x27;s developer experience.</p></div></div><div class="flex flex-col gap-20"><h3 class="typography text-body-xl font-montreal">Which payment methods do you accept?</h3><div class="text-[16px]"><p>We accept all major credit and debit cards. Your payment info is stored and processed securely by <a class="underline" href="https://stripe.com/">Stripe</a> and never touches our servers.</p></div></div><div class="flex flex-col gap-20"><h3 class="typography text-body-xl font-montreal">Why was I charged $1 after adding a credit card?</h3><div class="text-[16px]"><p>A $1 USD transaction is performed as a credit security check to ensure your card details are correct and authorized. The charge is refunded after the transaction completes.</p></div></div><div class="flex flex-col gap-20"><h3 class="typography text-body-xl font-montreal">I have an infrequently asked question.</h3><div class="text-[16px]"><p>Great! We&#x27;re always around to help. You can email us any time at <a class="underline" href="mailto:support@render.com">support@render.com</a>, or talk to us in our <a class="underline" href="https://community.render.com/">user community</a>.</p></div></div><script type="application/ld+json">{"@context":"https://schema.org","@type":"FAQPage","mainEntity":[{"@type":"Question","name":"How does billing work?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"Render charges for three things: your workspace plan (flat subscription), metered features like bandwidth (usage-based), and compute for your applications (usage-based).
